sources:
  +VBUS_IN    20.0 V  limit 3.00 A  load 1.319 A  (44%)  — USB-C PD sink contract 20 V / 3 A at the receptacle (pd_input J1; +VIN sits behind the TPS26631 eFuse, round 5)
  +3V3_SC      3.3 V  limit 0.30 A  load 0.022 A  (8%)  — SoM TPS7A20 always-on SC LDO U13 (J1.37); 300 mA class — the SoM power_architecture sheet says '3V3 (300mA)'. Envelope shared with the SoM-side SC (STM32G431 ~50 mA); carrier tally only here
  +5V_DBG      5.0 V  limit 0.50 A  load 0.045 A  (9%)  — debug USB-C VBUS (usb_jtag_connector J1) — host-supplied 5 V / 0.5 A USB2 default; feeds the usb_jtag AP2112K-3.3 debug-island LDO; isolated from carrier +5V

  DECAP board_services:U2.VBACKUP (V_RTC_BAT): VBACKUP is the RV-3028 coin-cell backup input (a rechargeable ML1220, not a switching rail); the RTC regulates internally and a cap on the cell net is optional — no bypass fitted by design
  I2C ZYNQ_HDMI_TX_SCL (typed on hdmi_tx): DDC pull-ups integrated in TPD12S016 (DS 7.3.9/7.3.15)
  I2C ZYNQ_HDMI_TX_SDA (typed on hdmi_tx): DDC pull-ups integrated in TPD12S016 (DS 7.3.9/7.3.15)
  RESET CP2102N_RST_N (on uart_bridge): open-drain RST: 1k pull-up only, internal POR; no RC cap
  RESET DBG_RST_N (on usb_jtag): CH347 RST#: 10k pull-up + the chip's built-in power-on reset (DS 5.1); no external RC cap fitted by design
  RESET LCD_CTP_RST (on lcd): GPIO-driven reset, held by 100k pull-down until PL releases
  RESET STM32_NRST (on debug_boot): internal-pull whitelist (part provides its own pull; external RC optional)
  RESET WATCHDOG_RST_N (on board_services): TPS3823 RESET# is a push-pull supervisor OUTPUT driving a PL bank-33 input as a firmware-mediated event (not a POR line): no pull needed (push-pull; PL internal pull holds it when +3V3_AUX is OFF), no cap by design (logic-event edge)
